-- NAPHUB · 99 คืนในป่า · เมนูและฟังก์ชันภาษาไทย [ปรับปรุงระบบตัดไม้และสำรวจแผนที่]
-- แก้ไขจากต้นฉบับ NAPHUB UI: เพิ่มระบบสวิตช์ / สไลเดอร์ / ดรอปดาวน์ / ปุ่ม ที่ใช้งานได้จริง
-- รูปแบบเมนู: ผู้เล่น · ฟาร์ม · ดึงของและย้าย · เทเลพอร์ต · อัตโนมัติ · ตั้งค่า
-- ชื่อ Remote / โฟลเดอร์ อ้างอิงจากไฟล์ Dump (PlaceId 126509999114328)
-- หมายเหตุ: ไฟล์ Dump ไม่มีอาร์กิวเมนต์ของ Remote และไม่ได้ดึง Workspace.Items มา
--           ฟังก์ชันที่เรียก Remote จึงห่อด้วย pcall ทั้งหมด ถ้าเกมอัปเดตแล้วใช้ไม่ได้ ให้ปรับที่หมวด "ตัวช่วยเกม"

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local VirtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- กันรันซ้ำ: ถ้ามีเมนูเก่าอยู่ให้ปิดก่อน
pcall(function()
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    if env.NAPHUB_UI and env.NAPHUB_UI.Shutdown then env.NAPHUB_UI.Shutdown() end
end)

local function hex(h)
    return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(h:sub(1, 2), 16), tonumber(h:sub(3, 4), 16), tonumber(h:sub(5, 6), 16))
end

local ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local LOOKUP = {}
for i = 1, #ALPHABET do LOOKUP[ALPHABET:byte(i)] = i - 1 end

local function b64decode(s)
    s = s:gsub("[^%w%+/=]", "")
    local out, n = {}, 0
    local buf, bn = {}, 0
    for i = 1, #s, 4 do
        local a, b, c, d = s:byte(i, i + 3)
        local v = (LOOKUP[a] or 0) * 262144 + (LOOKUP[b] or 0) * 4096 + (LOOKUP[c] or 0) * 64 + (LOOKUP[d] or 0)
        local b1 = math.floor(v / 65536) % 256
        local b2 = math.floor(v / 256) % 256
        local b3 = v % 256
        bn += 1
        if c == 61 then
            buf[bn] = string.char(b1)
        elseif d == 61 then
            buf[bn] = string.char(b1, b2)
        else
            buf[bn] = string.char(b1, b2, b3)
        end
        if bn >= 2000 then
            n += 1
            out[n] = table.concat(buf, "", 1, bn)
            buf, bn = {}, 0
        end
    end
    if bn > 0 then
        n += 1
        out[n] = table.concat(buf, "", 1, bn)
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

local function loadAsset(b64, path)
    if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return nil end
    local ok, asset = pcall(function()
        if not (isfile and isfile(path)) then
            writefile(path, b64decode(b64))
        end
        return getcustomasset(path)
    end)
    if ok and asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    return nil
end

local function loadBackground()
    if IMAGE_ASSET_ID ~= "" then return IMAGE_ASSET_ID end
    return loadAsset(BG_B64, "NAPHUB_bg_v3.jpg")
end

local function loadLogo()
    if LOGO_ASSET_ID ~= "" then return LOGO_ASSET_ID end
    return loadAsset(LOGO_B64, "NAPHUB_logo_v2.jpg")
end


----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ตัวช่วยสร้าง Instance
----------------------------------------------------------------
local alive = true
local conns = {}
local function track(c) conns[#conns + 1] = c; return c end

local function new(class, props, children)
    local inst = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do inst[k] = v end
    for _, child in ipairs(children or {}) do child.Parent = inst end
    return inst
end

local function corner(r)
    return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = type(r) == "number" and UDim.new(0, r) or r })
end

local function stroke(color, thickness)
    return new("UIStroke", { Color = color, Thickness = thickness or 2, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border })
end

local function list(padding, horizontal, halign, valign)
    return new("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, padding or 0),
        FillDirection = horizontal and Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal or Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        HorizontalAlignment = halign or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
        VerticalAlignment = valign or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    })
end

local function padding(l, t, r, b)
    return new("UIPadding", {
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or 0), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or 0),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or 0), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or 0),
    })
end

local function label(props)
    return new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Font = props.Font or FONT_BODY,
        TextSize = props.TextSize or 12,
        TextColor3 = props.Color or C.Text,
        TextXAlignment = props.Align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        Text = props.Text or "",
        Size = props.Size or UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Position = props.Position or UDim2.new(),
        AnchorPoint = props.AnchorPoint or Vector2.new(),
        LayoutOrder = props.LayoutOrder or 0,
        AutomaticSize = props.AutomaticSize or Enum.AutomaticSize.None,
        Parent = props.Parent,
    })
end

local function box(props, fill, outline, radius, thickness)
    local class = props.Class or "Frame"
    props.Class = nil
    local f = new(class, props)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = fill or C.Panel
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    if radius then corner(radius).Parent = f end
    if outline then stroke(outline, thickness or 2).Parent = f end
    return f
end

local function hitButton(parent, z)
    return new("TextButton", {
        Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = z or 9, Parent = parent,
    })
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ScreenGui
----------------------------------------------------------------
local gui = new("ScreenGui", {
    Name = "NAPHUB_99Nights",
    ResetOnSpawn = false,
    IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
})
if not pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
    gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

local root = new("Frame", {
    Name = "Window",
    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W.Width, W.Height),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Parent = gui,
})

box({
    Name = "Shadow",
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W.Shadow, W.Shadow),
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
    BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, Parent = root,
}, C.Shadow, nil, W.Radius)

local body = box({
    Name = "Body",
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
    ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = root,
}, C.Body, C.Frame, W.Radius, W.Stroke)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- เอฟเฟกต์สายฟ้าดำรอบกรอบเมนู (เรืองแสง + ไฟวิ่งรอบขอบ + สายฟ้าแลบสุ่ม)
-- ปิดทั้งหมดได้โดยตั้ง LIGHTNING = false
do
    local LIGHTNING = true
    local BOLT_CORE = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)         -- แกนสายฟ้า (ดำ)
    local BOLT_GLOW = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)   -- ขอบแสงจาง ๆ รอบสายฟ้า (เทา) ให้เห็นสายฟ้าดำบนพื้นดำ
    local BW, BH = W.Width - W.Shadow, W.Height - W.Shadow

    -- 1) เส้นขอบเป็นสีดำ มีแสงสว่างวิ่งวนรอบกรอบ
    local bodyStroke = body:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if bodyStroke and LIGHTNING then
        bodyStroke.Color = C.White
        bodyStroke.Thickness = 2
        local g = new("UIGradient", {
            Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 110)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            }),
            Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.55),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55),
            }),
            Parent = bodyStroke,
        })
        local conn
        conn = track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not gui.Parent then
                conn:Disconnect()
                return
            end
            g.Rotation = (os.clock() * 140) % 360
        end))
    end

    if LIGHTNING then

    -- 2) เงาดำด้านนอกกรอบ (กะพริบเบา ๆ)
    local glow = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Glow", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
        ZIndex = 0, Parent = root,
    }, { corner(W.Radius) })
    local gs = new("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 6, Transparency = 0.35,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = glow,
    })
    TweenService:Create(gs, TweenInfo.new(1.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
        Transparency = 0.88,
    }):Play()

    -- 3) สายฟ้าแลบตามขอบกรอบ
    local layer = new("Frame", {
        Name = "Lightning", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Shadow, 1, -W.Shadow),
        ZIndex = 10, Parent = root,
    })

    local function seg(parent, a, b, thick, color, transp)
        local d = b - a
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = transp
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude + thick * 0.5, thick)
        local m = (a + b) / 2
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(m.X, m.Y)
        f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end

    -- ขอบทั้ง 4: จุดเริ่ม + ทิศทาง (เดินตามเข็มนาฬิกา)
    local edges = {
        { Vector2.new(0, 0),   Vector2.new(1, 0),  BW },
        { Vector2.new(BW, 0),  Vector2.new(0, 1),  BH },
        { Vector2.new(BW, BH), Vector2.new(-1, 0), BW },
        { Vector2.new(0, BH),  Vector2.new(0, -1), BH },
    }

    local function drawPath(parent, pts, thickGlow, thickCore)
        for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
            seg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], thickGlow, BOLT_GLOW, 0.6)
            seg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], thickCore, BOLT_CORE, 0)
        end
    end

    local function spawnBolt()
        local r = math.random() * (2 * (BW + BH))
        local idx = 4
        for i = 1, 4 do
            if r <= edges[i][3] then idx = i break end
            r -= edges[i][3]
        end
        local start, dir, elen = edges[idx][1], edges[idx][2], edges[idx][3]
        local normal = Vector2.new(dir.Y, -dir.X) -- ชี้ออกนอกกรอบ
        local L = math.random(70, 160)
        local t0 = math.random() * math.max(elen - L, 0)
        local n = math.max(math.floor(L / 8), 4)

        local holder = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 10, Parent = layer,
        })

        local pts = {}
        for i = 0, n do
            local off = (i == 0 or i == n) and 0 or (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 7
            pts[i + 1] = start + dir * (t0 + L * i / n) + normal * off
        end
        drawPath(holder, pts, 5, 1.5)

        -- กิ่งแยกสั้น ๆ
        if math.random() < 0.65 then
            local k = math.random(2, n)
            local side = (math.random() < 0.5) and -1 or 1
            local bdir = (normal * side + dir * (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.7).Unit
            local bp = { pts[k] }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                bp[i + 1] = bp[i] + bdir * 8 + Vector2.new(math.random() * 6 - 3, math.random() * 6 - 3)
            end
            drawPath(holder, bp, 4, 1)
        end

        -- กะพริบแล้วหายไป
        task.delay(0.06, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = false end end)
        task.delay(0.10, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = true end end)
        task.delay(0.20, function() holder:Destroy() end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while gui.Parent do
            task.wait(0.08 + math.random() * 0.22)
            if gui.Parent and root.Visible then
                pcall(spawnBolt)
                if math.random() < 0.4 then pcall(spawnBolt) end
            end
        end
    end)
    end -- if LIGHTNING
end

-- รูปพื้นหลัง + ดาว
local bgAsset = loadBackground()
if bgAsset then
    local bgImg = new("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "Background", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = bgAsset,
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -W.Ground + IMAGE_OFFSET_Y),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(IMAGE_W, IMAGE_H), ZIndex = 1, Parent = body,
    })
    new("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 45,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.55),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.35),
        }),
        Parent = bgImg,
    })
end

do
local stars = new("Frame", {
    Name = "Stars", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
    ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 1, Parent = body,
})
for _ = 1, 18 do
    local sz = math.random(1, 2)
    local d = box({
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.random(), math.random()), ZIndex = 1, Parent = stars,
    }, C.White, nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    task.delay(math.random() * 2, function()
        if d.Parent then
            TweenService:Create(d, TweenInfo.new(1 + math.random() * 1.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
            }):Play()
        end
    end)
end

local ANGLE = 30
local function shootingStar()
    local len = math.random(50, 95)
    local startX = math.random(math.floor(W.Width * 0.35), W.Width + 40)
    local startY = math.random(-30, math.floor(W.Height * 0.4))
    local dist = math.random(240, 380)
    local dx = dist * math.cos(math.rad(ANGLE))
    local dy = dist * math.sin(math.rad(ANGLE))
    local t = 0.8 + math.random() * 0.7
    local star = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = C.White, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, 2),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(startX, startY), Rotation = -ANGLE, ZIndex = 1, Parent = stars,
    })
    corner(UDim.new(1, 0)).Parent = star
    new("UIGradient", {
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }),
        Parent = star,
    })
    TweenService:Create(star, TweenInfo.new(t, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(startX - dx, startY + dy),
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(star, TweenInfo.new(t * 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, t * 0.55), {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }):Play()
    task.delay(t + 0.1, function() star:Destroy() end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while alive and gui.Parent do
        task.wait(0.7 + math.random() * 1.3)
        if alive and gui.Parent and root.Visible then pcall(shootingStar) end
    end
end)

end

-- ลากหน้าต่าง (เมาส์ + ทัช)
do
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    track(topbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = root.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
            end)
        end
    end))
    track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            root.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end))
end

    Player = "⌂", Farm = "✿", Pull = "⇄", Teleport = "➤", Auto = "⚙",
    Settings = "◈", Combat = "⚔", Visuals = "◉", Heal = "✚",

local function iconBadge(parent, x, y, size, glyph, active, z)
    z = z or 4
    local badge = box({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), ZIndex = z, Parent = parent,
    }, active and C.Accent or C.SidebarAlt, (not active) and C.Outline or nil, 6, 1)
    label({
        Text = glyph, Font = FONT, TextSize = size - 9,
        Color = active and C.AccentInk or C.White, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = badge,
    }).ZIndex = z + 1
    return badge
end

local pages, tabs, currentPage = {}, {}, nil

local function lockScroll(locked)
    if currentPage then currentPage.Scroll.ScrollingEnabled = not locked end
end

local mainArea = new("Frame", {
    Name = "Main", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W.Sidebar, W.Topbar),
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -W.Sidebar, 1, -(W.Topbar + W.Ground)), ZIndex = 2, Parent = body,
})
local pageTitle = label({
    Font = FONT, TextSize = 16, Color = C.White,
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 20), Parent = mainArea,
})
local pageDesc = label({
    TextSize = 11, Color = C.SubText,
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 23), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 14), Parent = mainArea,
})

local function selectTab(name)
    for n, p in pairs(pages) do
        p.Scroll.Visible = (n == name)
        local t = tabs[n]
        if t then
            local on = (n == name)
            t.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1
            t.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = C.TabActive
            local s = t.Frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if s then s.Enabled = on end
            t.Badge.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.Accent or C.SidebarAlt
            local lb = t.Badge:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if lb then lb.TextColor3 = on and C.AccentInk or C.White end
        end
    end
    currentPage = pages[name]
    if currentPage then
        pageTitle.Text = currentPage.Title
        pageDesc.Text = currentPage.Desc
    end
end

local function makePage(order, key, iconKey, title, desc)
    -- ปุ่มแท็บ
    local b = box({
        Name = "Tab_" .. key, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        LayoutOrder = order, ZIndex = 3, Parent = tabHolder,
    }, C.TabActive, C.Line, 7, 1)
    b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Enabled = false
    local badge = iconBadge(b, 4, 4, 22, ICON[iconKey] or iconKey, false, 4)
    label({
        Text = title, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0), Parent = b,
    }).ZIndex = 4
    local hit = hitButton(b, 6)
    -- หน้าเนื้อหา
    local scroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = "Page_" .. key, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, W.Header), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -W.Header),
        ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.Line,
        ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), Visible = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = mainArea,
    })
    local function column(xScale, xOffset)
        return new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(xScale, xOffset, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -14, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ZIndex = 2, Parent = scroll,
        }, { list(8) })
    end
    local colL, colR = column(0, 10), column(0.5, 4)
    local function updateCanvas()
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(colL.AbsoluteSize.Y, colR.AbsoluteSize.Y) + 14)
    end
    colL:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateCanvas)
    colR:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateCanvas)
    updateCanvas()
    pages[key] = { Scroll = scroll, Left = colL, Right = colR, Title = title, Desc = desc }
    tabs[key] = { Frame = b, Badge = badge }
    hit.Activated:Connect(function() selectTab(key) end)
    return pages[key]
end

local function maskName(n) -- แสดงชื่อแบบปิดบังบางส่วน เช่น op*****
    if #n <= 2 then return n end
    return n:sub(1, 2) .. string.rep("*", math.min(#n - 2, 5))
end

    Text = maskName(LocalPlayer.DisplayName), Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White,

    toastId += 1
    local id = toastId
    toastLbl.Text = text
    toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    toast.Visible = true
    task.delay(2.2, function()
        if id == toastId and toast.Parent then toast.Visible = false end
    end)
end

local HEAD = 30

-- กล่องกลุ่ม สูงอัตโนมัติตามจำนวนแถว
local function group(parent, order, title, iconKey)
    local card = box({
        Name = "Group_" .. title, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, LayoutOrder = order, ZIndex = 2, Parent = parent, ClipsDescendants = true,
    }, C.Panel, C.Outline, 9, 1)
    new("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = card })
    local head = box({
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, HEAD), BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, LayoutOrder = 0, ZIndex = 3, Parent = card,
    }, C.PanelHeader)
    box({ AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), ZIndex = 4, Parent = head }, C.Outline)
    iconBadge(head, 6, 5, 20, ICON[iconKey] or iconKey or "◈", true, 4)
    label({
        Text = title, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0), Parent = head,
    }).ZIndex = 4
    return new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 3, Parent = card,
    }, { list(5), padding(10, 8, 10, 8) })
end

local order = 0
local function nextOrder() order += 1; return order end

local function safe(cb, ...)
    if not cb then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(cb, ...)
    if not ok then warn("[NAPHUB] " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- สวิตช์
local function toggle(parent, text, default, cb)
    local row = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
    })
    label({ TextSize = 12, Color = C.Text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0), Parent = row }).Text = text
    local state = default and true or false
    local trk = box({
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 18), ZIndex = 4, Parent = row,
    }, C.Track, C.Line, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
    local knob = box({
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 5, Parent = trk,
    }, C.White, nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local function render()
        trk.BackgroundColor3 = state and C.Accent or C.Track
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = state and C.AccentInk or C.White
        TweenService:Create(knob, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
            Position = state and UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0),
        }):Play()
    end
    render()
    local api = {}
    function api.Set(v, silent)
        state = v and true or false
        render()
        if not silent then task.spawn(safe, cb, state) end
    end
    function api.Get() return state end
    hitButton(row, 8).Activated:Connect(function() api.Set(not state) end)
    if state then task.spawn(safe, cb, state) end
    return api
end

-- สไลเดอร์ (ลากได้ ทั้งเมาส์และทัช)
local function slider(parent, text, min, max, default, cb)
    local row = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
    })
    label({ TextSize = 12, Color = C.Text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 18), Parent = row }).Text = text
    local valLbl = label({
        Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, Color = C.SubText, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 18), Parent = row,
    })
    local trk = box({
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8), ZIndex = 4, Parent = row,
    }, C.Track, C.Outline, UDim.new(1, 0), 1)
    local fill = box({ Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), ZIndex = 5, Parent = trk }, C.Accent, nil, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local knob = box({
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 6, Parent = trk,
    }, C.White, C.Black, UDim.new(1, 0), 2)
    local value = default
    local function render()
        local a = (value - min) / (max - min)
        fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(a, 1)
        knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(a, 0.5)
        valLbl.Text = tostring(value)
    end
    render()
    local function setFromX(x)
        local a = math.clamp((x - trk.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(trk.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
        local v = math.floor(min + a * (max - min) + 0.5)
        if v ~= value then
            value = v; render()
            task.spawn(safe, cb, value)
        end
    end
    local hit = new("TextButton", {
        Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-6, 14), Size = UDim2.new(1, 12, 0, 28), ZIndex = 8, Parent = row,
    })
    local dragging = false
    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true; lockScroll(true); setFromX(input.Position.X)
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false; lockScroll(false) end
            end)
        end
    end)
    track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            setFromX(input.Position.X)
        end
    end))
    local api = {}
    function api.Get() return value end
    function api.Set(v) value = math.clamp(v, min, max); render(); task.spawn(safe, cb, value) end
    return api
end

-- ดรอปดาวน์ (เปิดรายการใต้ช่อง) ตัวเลือกเป็น string หรือ {text=, value=} หรือฟังก์ชันที่คืนรายการ
-- multi = true: เลือกได้หลายอัน (กดซ้ำเพื่อยกเลิก, แผงไม่ปิด) / api.GetAll() คืนรายการค่าที่เลือก
local function dropdown(parent, text, options, cb, placeholder, multi)
    local row = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
    }, { list(4) })
    label({ TextSize = 12, Color = C.Text, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = 0, Parent = row }).Text = text
    local head = box({
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 4, Parent = row,
    }, C.Track, C.Line, 7, 1)
    local valLbl = label({
        Text = placeholder or "...", TextSize = 11, Color = C.Text,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0), Parent = head,
    })
    local arrow = label({
        Text = "▾", Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 18), Parent = head,
    })
    local panel = box({
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 2, Visible = false, ZIndex = 4, Parent = row,
    }, C.Track, C.Outline, 7, 1)
    local sc = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.Line, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 5, Parent = panel,
    }, { list(2), padding(4, 4, 4, 4) })
    local selected = nil
    local selMap, selOrder = {}, {}
    local api = {}
    local function updateLbl()
        if #selOrder == 0 then valLbl.Text = placeholder or "..."
        elseif #selOrder == 1 then valLbl.Text = selMap[selOrder[1]].text
        else valLbl.Text = "เลือกแล้ว " .. #selOrder .. " รายการ" end
    end
    local function getList()
        local raw = type(options) == "function" and options() or options
        local out = {}
        for _, o in ipairs(raw or {}) do
            if type(o) == "table" then out[#out + 1] = o else out[#out + 1] = { text = tostring(o), value = o } end
        end
        return out
    end
    local function build()
        for _, c in ipairs(sc:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("TextButton") or c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end end
        local items = getList()
        if #items == 0 then
            label({ Text = "(ไม่มีรายการ)", TextSize = 11, Color = C.Muted, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), Parent = sc })
        end
        for i, o in ipairs(items) do
            local b = new("TextButton", {
                Text = "  " .. o.text, Font = FONT_BODY, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = C.Text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = C.Element, BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = i, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6, Parent = sc,
            }, { corner(5) })
            -- รายการที่เลือก (โหมดหลายอัน) แสดงเป็นสีเลือกแทนเครื่องหมาย
            local function paint()
                local on = multi and selMap[o.value] ~= nil
                b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 87) or C.Element
                b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 0.2
            end
            paint()
            b.Activated:Connect(function()
                if multi then
                    if selMap[o.value] then
                        selMap[o.value] = nil
                        for i2, v2 in ipairs(selOrder) do if v2 == o.value then table.remove(selOrder, i2); break end end
                    else
                        selMap[o.value] = o
                        selOrder[#selOrder + 1] = o.value
                    end
                    paint()
                    updateLbl()
                    task.spawn(safe, cb, api.GetAll())
                    return
                end
                selected = o
                valLbl.Text = o.text
                panel.Visible = false; arrow.Text = "▾"
                task.spawn(safe, cb, o.value, o.text)
            end)
        end
        local n = math.min(math.max(#items, 1), 5)
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, n * 24 + 8)
    end
    function api.Refresh() if panel.Visible then build() end end
    function api.Get()
        if multi then return selOrder[1] end
        return selected and selected.value or nil
    end
    function api.GetAll()
        if multi then return table.clone(selOrder) end
        local v = selected and selected.value
        return v ~= nil and { v } or {}
    end
    function api.GetText() return selected and selected.text or nil end
    function api.Set(value)
        for _, o in ipairs(getList()) do
            if o.value == value or o.text == value then
                if multi then
                    if not selMap[o.value] then selMap[o.value] = o; selOrder[#selOrder + 1] = o.value end
                    updateLbl()
                else
                    selected = o; valLbl.Text = o.text
                end
                return
            end
        end
    end
    function api.Clear()
        selected = nil; selMap, selOrder = {}, {}
        valLbl.Text = placeholder or "..."
    end
    hitButton(head, 8).Activated:Connect(function()
        panel.Visible = not panel.Visible
        arrow.Text = panel.Visible and "▴" or "▾"
        if panel.Visible then build() end
    end)
    return api
end

-- ปุ่ม
local function button(parent, text, cb)
    local wrap = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
    })
    box({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), ZIndex = 3, Parent = wrap }, C.Shadow, C.Outline, 7, 1)
    local face = box({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), ZIndex = 4, Parent = wrap }, C.Element, C.Line, 7, 1)
    label({
        Text = text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, Color = C.White, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0), Parent = face,
    }).ZIndex = 5
    local h = hitButton(face, 8)
    h.Activated:Connect(function()
        face.BackgroundColor3 = C.Hover
        task.delay(0.12, function() if face.Parent then face.BackgroundColor3 = C.Element end end)
        task.spawn(safe, cb)
    end)
end

-- แถวข้อความข้อมูล
local function info(parent, text)
    local l = label({
        TextSize = 11, Color = C.SubText, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        Parent = parent,
    })
    l.TextWrapped = true
    l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
    l.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
    l.Text = text
    return l
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ตัวช่วยเกม (อ้างอิงชื่อจากไฟล์ Dump)
----------------------------------------------------------------
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local RE = RS:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvents")

-- เรียก Remote แบบปลอดภัย (RemoteEvent = FireServer, RemoteFunction = InvokeServer)
local function callRemote(name, ...)
    local r = RE and RE:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not r then return false end
    local ok, res = pcall(function(...)
        if r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return r:InvokeServer(...) end
        r:FireServer(...)
        return true
    end, ...)
    return ok, res
end

local function getChar() return LocalPlayer.Character end
local function getHum()
    local c = getChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end
local function getHRP()
    local c = getChar()
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function posOf(inst)
    if not inst then return nil end
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.Position end
    if inst:IsA("Model") then
        local ok, cf = pcall(function() return inst:GetPivot() end)
        if ok then return cf.Position end
    end
    return nil
end

local function dist(a, b) return (a - b).Magnitude end

local function tpTo(cf)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp and cf then hrp.CFrame = cf + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0) end
end

local function rnd(a) return (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * a end

-- โฟลเดอร์ของเกม
local function itemsFolder() return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Items") end
local function charactersFolder() return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters") end
local function mapFolder() return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") end

local fireCache
local function getFire()
    if fireCache and fireCache.Parent then return fireCache end
    local map = mapFolder()
    if not map then return nil end
    local camp = map:FindFirstChild("Campground")
    if camp then
        local f = camp:FindFirstChild("MainFire") or camp:FindFirstChild("Campfire") or camp:FindFirstChild("Fire")
        if f then fireCache = f; return f end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
        if (d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("BasePart")) and (d.Name == "MainFire" or d.Name == "Campfire") then
            fireCache = d; return d
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- [แก้] ตำแหน่งกองไฟ: ใช้ MainFire.Center (จุดไฟจริง) แทน Pivot ของโมเดล เพราะ Pivot ของ MainFire มักไม่ได้อยู่ตรงกลางกองไฟ
--       ถ้ายังเพี้ยน กดปุ่ม "ตั้งตำแหน่งกองไฟ = ที่ที่ยืนอยู่" (หน้าดึงของ) เพื่อกำหนดเองได้
local fireOverride = nil
local function firePos()
    if fireOverride then return fireOverride end
    local fire = getFire()
    if not fire then return nil end
    for _, n in ipairs({ "Center", "InnerTouchZone", "OuterTouchZone" }) do
        local c = fire:FindFirstChild(n)
        if c and c:IsA("BasePart") then return c.Position end
    end
    return posOf(fire)
end

-- ตำแหน่งโต๊ะคราฟ: ค่าเฉลี่ยของ Part ที่เป็นลูกตรง ๆ ของ Campground.CraftingBench (ไม่รวมกองเพชรที่เป็นโมเดลย่อย)
local function benchPos()
    local map = mapFolder()
    local cg = map and map:FindFirstChild("Campground")
    local bench = cg and cg:FindFirstChild("CraftingBench")
    if not bench then return nil end
    local sum, n = Vector3.zero, 0
    for _, c in ipairs(bench:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("BasePart") then sum += c.Position; n += 1 end
    end
    if n > 0 then return sum / n end
    return posOf(bench)
end

local function lowerEq(a, b) return a:lower() == b:lower() end

-- ชื่อที่ตรงกัน: key เป็น string หรือ table ของชื่อ ถ้าขึ้นต้นด้วย "~" = ค้นหาแบบมีคำนี้อยู่ในชื่อ
local function nameMatches(name, key)
    if type(key) == "table" then
        for _, k in ipairs(key) do if nameMatches(name, k) then return true end end
        return false
    end
    if key:sub(1, 1) == "~" then return name:lower():find(key:sub(2):lower(), 1, true) ~= nil end
    return lowerEq(name, key)
end

local function findItems(key)
    local out, f = {}, itemsFolder()
    if not f then return out end
    for _, it in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
        if nameMatches(it.Name, key) then out[#out + 1] = it end
    end
    return out
end

-- ย้ายไอเทม: เริ่มลาก -> วางตำแหน่ง -> หยุดลาก
local function moveItem(item, cf)
    if not (item and item.Parent) then return end
    callRemote("RequestStartDraggingItem", item)
    pcall(function() item:PivotTo(cf) end)
    task.wait(0.03)
    pcall(function() item:PivotTo(cf) end)
    callRemote("StopDraggingItem", item)
end

-- จุดปลายทางการดึง
local pullTargetName = "ตัวละคร"
local function destinationCF(target)
    if (target or pullTargetName) == "กองไฟ" then
        local p = firePos()
        if p then return CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(rnd(1.5), 3, rnd(1.5))), p, 6 end
        return nil
    end
    if (target or pullTargetName) == "โต๊ะคราฟ" then
        local p = benchPos()
        if p then return CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(rnd(1.5), 4, rnd(1.5))), p, 6 end
        return nil
    end
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp then
        return hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(rnd(3), 1, -5 + rnd(2)), hrp.Position, 8
    end
    return nil
end

-- [แก้] เติมเชื้อเพลิง: เกมนี้ไม่ได้นับของที่แค่ "ลอยเหนือกองไฟ" ต้องยิง RequestBurnItem(MainFire, ไอเทม) ตามที่เห็นใน Remote Log
local FUEL_SET = { ["log"] = true, ["coal"] = true, ["fuel canister"] = true, ["oil barrel"] = true, ["biofuel"] = true }
local function isFuelKey(key)
    if type(key) == "table" then
        for _, k in ipairs(key) do if isFuelKey(k) then return true end end
        return false
    end
    return FUEL_SET[tostring(key):lower()] == true
end

-- เกมไม่เช็คระยะผู้เล่นกับกองไฟ -> ไม่ต้องวาร์ป เรียกทำงานตรง ๆ
local function nearFire(fn)
    local ok, res = pcall(fn)
    return ok and res or 0
end

local function burnItem(item)
    local fire, fp = getFire(), firePos()
    if not (fire and item and item.Parent) then return false end
    callRemote("RequestStartDraggingItem", item)
    if fp then pcall(function() item:PivotTo(CFrame.new(fp + Vector3.new(rnd(0.8), 2.5, rnd(0.8)))) end) end
    task.wait(0.05)
    callRemote("RequestBurnItem", fire, item)
    callRemote("StopDraggingItem", item)
    return true
end

local pullCount = 10
local function pullItems(key, count, target)
    local _, center, skipR = destinationCF(target)
    if not center then notify("ไม่พบจุดปลายทาง"); return 0 end
    local burnMode = (target or pullTargetName) == "กองไฟ" and isFuelKey(key)
    if burnMode then
        local found = findItems(key)
        local fp = center
        table.sort(found, function(a, b)
            return dist(posOf(a) or Vector3.zero, fp) < dist(posOf(b) or Vector3.zero, fp)
        end)
        return nearFire(function()
            local n = 0
            for _, it in ipairs(found) do
                if n >= (count or pullCount) then break end
                if it.Parent and burnItem(it) then n += 1; task.wait(0.08) end
            end
            return n
        end)
    end
    local found = findItems(key)
    table.sort(found, function(a, b)
        local pa, pb = posOf(a), posOf(b)
        return (pa and dist(pa, center) or 1e9) < (pb and dist(pb, center) or 1e9)
    end)
    local n = 0
    for _, it in ipairs(found) do
        if n >= (count or pullCount) then break end
        local p = posOf(it)
        if p and dist(p, center) > skipR then
            local cf = destinationCF(target)
            if cf then moveItem(it, cf); n += 1 end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end
    return n
end

-- เครื่องมือในตัว
-- หมายเหตุ: เกมนี้เก็บเครื่องมือไว้ที่ Players.<ชื่อ>.Inventory (ไม่ใช่ Backpack) ตามไฟล์ Remote Log
local function inventoryFolder() return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Inventory") end

local function allTools()
    local t = {}
    local inv = inventoryFolder()
    if inv then for _, x in ipairs(inv:GetChildren()) do t[#t + 1] = x end end
    local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if bp then for _, x in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if x:IsA("Tool") then t[#t + 1] = x end end end
    local c = getChar()
    if c then for _, x in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do if x:IsA("Tool") then t[#t + 1] = x end end end
    return t
end

-- หาเครื่องมือ: ลองชื่อตรงเป๊ะก่อน (exactOnly = true จะไม่ใช้แบบมีคำนี้อยู่ในชื่อ)
local function findTool(patterns, exactOnly)
    local tools = allTools()
    for _, p in ipairs(patterns) do
        for _, t in ipairs(tools) do
            if t.Name:lower() == p:lower() then return t end
        end
    end
    if exactOnly then return nil end
    for _, p in ipairs(patterns) do
        for _, t in ipairs(tools) do
            if t.Name:lower():find(p:lower(), 1, true) then return t end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local equipT = 0
local function equip(tool)
    if not tool then return nil end
    local c = getChar()
    if c and c:GetAttribute("Equipped") == tool.Name then return tool end
    if os.clock() - equipT < 0.8 then return tool end
    equipT = os.clock()
    if tool.Parent == inventoryFolder() then
        callRemote("EquipItemHandle", "FireAllClients", tool)
    else
        local hum = getHum()
        if hum and tool.Parent ~= c then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
    end
    task.wait(0.1)
    return tool
end

local hitCounter = 0
local function nextHitId()
    hitCounter += 1
    return tostring(hitCounter) .. "_" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
end

-- ตีวัตถุ/ศัตรู ผ่าน ToolDamageObject (รูปแบบอาร์กิวเมนต์ตาม Remote Log: เป้าหมาย, เครื่องมือ, รหัสการตี, CFrame ผู้เล่น, flag)
local function damage(target, tool, flag)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if not (target and tool and hrp) then return end
    local id, cf = nextHitId(), hrp.CFrame
    task.spawn(function()
        callRemote("ToolDamageObject", target, tool, id, cf, flag and true or false)
    end)
end
