----------------------------------------------------------------
-- หน้า: ตั้งค่า
----------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ค่าเริ่มต้น + รายการตัวเลือก
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local DEFAULTS = { theme = "ดำ", font = "Gotham", size = 13, bg = 10, mode = "Shared" }
    local cfg = { theme = DEFAULTS.theme, font = DEFAULTS.font, size = DEFAULTS.size, bg = DEFAULTS.bg }
    local saveMode = DEFAULTS.mode
    local loading = true -- กันไม่ให้เซฟระหว่างโหลด/รีเซ็ต

    local THEME_NAMES = { "ดำ", "เทาเข้ม", "น้ำเงินเข้ม", "เขียวเข้ม", "แดงเข้ม" }
    local FAMILIES = {
        Gotham     = { Bold = Enum.Font.GothamBold,     Body = Enum.Font.GothamMedium },
        Arial      = { Bold = Enum.Font.ArialBold,      Body = Enum.Font.Arial },
        SourceSans = { Bold = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, Body = Enum.Font.SourceSans },
        Ubuntu     = { Bold = Enum.Font.Ubuntu,         Body = Enum.Font.Ubuntu },
        Nunito     = { Bold = Enum.Font.Nunito,         Body = Enum.Font.Nunito },
        Roboto     = { Bold = Enum.Font.Roboto,         Body = Enum.Font.Roboto },
    }
    local FONT_NAMES = { "Gotham", "Arial", "SourceSans", "Ubuntu", "Nunito", "Roboto" }

    local themeHooks = {}

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- เปลี่ยนธีมสดๆ: แทนที่สีเก่า -> สีใหม่ ทั้งหน้าต่าง แล้วอัปเดตตาราง C
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local KEYMAP = {
        { "Body", "Body" }, { "Sidebar", "Sidebar" }, { "Topbar", "Topbar" }, { "SidebarAlt", "SidebarAlt" },
        { "PanelHeader", "PanelHeader" }, { "Element", "Element" }, { "Panel", "Panel" }, { "TabActive", "TabActive" },
        { "Hover", "Hover" }, { "Track", "Track" }, { "Outline", "Outline" }, { "Frame", "Frame" },
        { "Line", "Line" }, { "SubText", "SubText" }, { "Muted", "Muted" }, { "SidebarMuted", "SidebarMuted" },
        { "Accent", "Accent" }, { "AccentInk", "AccentDark" },
    }
    local function ckey(c)
        return math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5) * 65536 + math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5) * 256 + math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)
    end
    local function applyTheme(name)
        local np = PALETTES[name]
        if not np then return end
        local map = {}
        for _, kv in ipairs(KEYMAP) do
            local old, new = C[kv[1]], hex(np[kv[2]])
            local k = ckey(old)
            if ckey(new) ~= k and map[k] == nil then map[k] = new end
        end
        local function swap(inst, prop)
            local ok, cur = pcall(function() return inst[prop] end)
            if ok and typeof(cur) == "Color3" then
                local n = map[ckey(cur)]
                if n then pcall(function() inst[prop] = n end) end
            end
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
                swap(d, "BackgroundColor3"); swap(d, "BorderColor3")
                if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then swap(d, "TextColor3") end
                if d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then swap(d, "ImageColor3") end
                if d:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then swap(d, "ScrollBarImageColor3") end
            elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                swap(d, "Color")
            end
        end
        for _, kv in ipairs(KEYMAP) do C[kv[1]] = hex(np[kv[2]]) end
        for _, h in ipairs(themeHooks) do pcall(h) end
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ฟอนต์ / ขนาดตัวอักษร / ความโปร่งใสพื้นหลัง
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local function isText(d) return d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") end
    local function applyText(d)
        if not (d and d.Parent and isText(d)) then return end
        if d:GetAttribute("NB_Base") == nil then
            d:SetAttribute("NB_Base", d.TextSize)
            d:SetAttribute("NB_Bold", d.Font == Enum.Font.GothamBold)
        end
        local fam = FAMILIES[cfg.font] or FAMILIES.Gotham
        d.Font = d:GetAttribute("NB_Bold") and fam.Bold or fam.Body
        d.TextSize = math.max(8, math.floor(d:GetAttribute("NB_Base") * (cfg.size / DEFAULTS.size) + 0.5))
    end
    local function applyAllText()
        for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do applyText(d) end
    end
    local function applyBg()
        body.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(cfg.bg / 100, 0, 1)
    end
    -- ข้อความที่สร้างทีหลัง (เช่น รายการดรอปดาวน์) ให้ใช้ฟอนต์/ขนาดเดียวกัน
    track(gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if isText(d) then task.defer(applyText, d) end
    end))

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- เซฟ / โหลด (Shared = ใช้ร่วมกันทุกบัญชี, PerAccount = แยกตามบัญชี)
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local DIR = "NAPHUB_99Nights"
    local hasFS = type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function"
    local function pathFor(mode)
        return DIR .. "/" .. (mode == "PerAccount" and ("acc_" .. LocalPlayer.UserId) or "shared") .. ".json"
    end
    local function ensureDir()
        if not hasFS then return end
        pcall(function()
            if type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder(DIR) then makefolder(DIR) end
        end)
    end
    local function encode()
        return HttpService:JSONEncode({ theme = cfg.theme, font = cfg.font, size = cfg.size, bg = cfg.bg })
    end
    local function saveNow()
        if loading or not hasFS then return end
        ensureDir()
        pcall(writefile, pathFor(saveMode), encode())
        pcall(writefile, DIR .. "/mode.txt", saveMode)
    end

    -- ตรวจและรับค่าจากตาราง (ใช้ทั้งตอนโหลดไฟล์และตอนนำเข้า)
    local function takeConfig(t)
        if type(t) ~= "table" then return false end
        local ok = false
        if type(t.theme) == "string" and PALETTES[t.theme] then cfg.theme = t.theme; ok = true end
        if type(t.font) == "string" and FAMILIES[t.font] then cfg.font = t.font; ok = true end
        if type(t.size) == "number" then cfg.size = math.clamp(math.floor(t.size), 10, 18); ok = true end
        if type(t.bg) == "number" then cfg.bg = math.clamp(math.floor(t.bg), 0, 80); ok = true end
        return ok
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ตัวควบคุมใหม่: ปุ่มเลือก 2 ช่อง (Shared/PerAccount) และช่องกรอกข้อความ
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local function segmented(parent, text, opts, default, cb)
        local row = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
        }, { list(4) })
        label({ TextSize = 12, Color = C.Text, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = 0, Parent = row }).Text = text
        local bar = box({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 4, Parent = row }, C.Track, C.Line, 7, 1)
        new("UIListLayout", {
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 2),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = bar,
        })
        padding(2, 2, 2, 2).Parent = bar
        local btns, current = {}, default
        local function paint()
            for key, b in pairs(btns) do
                local on = key == current
                b.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.Accent or C.Track
                b.TextColor3 = on and C.AccentInk or C.Text
            end
        end
        for i, key in ipairs(opts) do
            local b = new("TextButton", {
                Text = key, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2.new(1 / #opts, -2, 1, 0), LayoutOrder = i, ZIndex = 5, Parent = bar,
            }, { corner(5) })
            btns[key] = b
            b.Activated:Connect(function()
                current = key; paint()
                task.spawn(safe, cb, key)
            end)
        end
        paint()
        themeHooks[#themeHooks + 1] = paint
        local api = {}
        function api.Set(k) current = k; paint() end
        function api.Get() return current end
        return api
    end

    local function textInput(parent, text, placeholder, cb)
        local row = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            LayoutOrder = nextOrder(), ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
        }, { list(4) })
        label({ TextSize = 12, Color = C.Text, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = 0, Parent = row }).Text = text
        local holder = box({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 4, Parent = row }, C.Track, C.Line, 7, 1)
        local tb = new("TextBox", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0),
            Text = "", PlaceholderText = placeholder, PlaceholderColor3 = C.Muted, TextColor3 = C.Text,
            Font = FONT_BODY, TextSize = 11, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = holder,
        })
        themeHooks[#themeHooks + 1] = function() tb.PlaceholderColor3 = C.Muted end
        tb.FocusLost:Connect(function() task.spawn(safe, cb, tb.Text, tb) end)
        return tb
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- หน้าตั้งค่า (ตามภาพ): ตั้งค่าความงาม | จัดการระบบ
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local ap = group(pgSet.Left, nextOrder(), "ตั้งค่าความงาม", "Light")
    local ddTheme = dropdown(ap, "ธีม", THEME_NAMES, function(v)
        cfg.theme = v; applyTheme(v); saveNow()
    end, "ดำ")
    local ddFont = dropdown(ap, "ฟอนต์", FONT_NAMES, function(v)
        cfg.font = v; applyAllText(); saveNow()
    end, "Gotham")
    local slSize = slider(ap, "ขนาดตัวอักษร", 10, 18, DEFAULTS.size, function(v)
        cfg.size = v; applyAllText(); saveNow()
    end)
    local slBg = slider(ap, "ความโปร่งใสพื้นหลัง", 0, 80, DEFAULTS.bg, function(v)
        cfg.bg = v; applyBg(); saveNow()
    end)

    local mg = group(pgSet.Right, nextOrder(), "จัดการระบบ", "Settings")
    local seg = segmented(mg, "การเซฟค่า", { "Shared", "PerAccount" }, saveMode, function(key)
        saveMode = key
        if hasFS then
            ensureDir()
            pcall(writefile, DIR .. "/mode.txt", saveMode)
            -- ถ้าโหมดนี้เคยมีไฟล์เซฟไว้ ให้โหลดมาใช้ ไม่งั้นเซฟค่าปัจจุบันลงไฟล์ของโหมดนี้
            local okR, raw = pcall(function()
                if isfile(pathFor(saveMode)) then return readfile(pathFor(saveMode)) end
            end)
            local okD, t = false, nil
            if okR and raw then okD, t = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end) end
            loading = true
            if okD and takeConfig(t) then
                applyTheme(cfg.theme); applyAllText(); applyBg()
                ddTheme.Set(cfg.theme); ddFont.Set(cfg.font); slSize.Set(cfg.size); slBg.Set(cfg.bg)
                loading = false
                notify("โหลดค่าโหมด " .. saveMode)
            else
                loading = false
                saveNow()
                notify("เซฟค่าแบบ " .. saveMode)
            end
        else
            notify("executor นี้ไม่รองรับการบันทึกไฟล์ (ใช้ได้แค่คัดลอก/นำเข้า)")
        end
    end)

    button(mg, "คัดลอกตั้งค่า", function()
        local text = encode()
        local clip = (type(setclipboard) == "function" and setclipboard) or (type(toclipboard) == "function" and toclipboard)
        if clip then
            local ok = pcall(clip, text)
            notify(ok and "คัดลอกตั้งค่าแล้ว" or "คัดลอกไม่สำเร็จ")
        else
            notify("executor ไม่รองรับคลิปบอร์ด")
        end
    end)

    local importBox
    importBox = textInput(mg, "นำเข้าตั้งค่า", "วางโค้ดตั้งค่าที่นี่...", function(text)
        text = (text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if text == "" then return end
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(text) end)
        loading = true
        if ok and takeConfig(t) then
            applyTheme(cfg.theme); applyAllText(); applyBg()
            ddTheme.Set(cfg.theme); ddFont.Set(cfg.font); slSize.Set(cfg.size); slBg.Set(cfg.bg)
            loading = false
            saveNow()
            notify("นำเข้าตั้งค่าแล้ว")
        else
            loading = false
            notify("โค้ดตั้งค่าไม่ถูกต้อง")
        end
        importBox.Text = ""
    end)

    button(mg, "ล้างการตั้งค่าทั้งหมด", function()
        loading = true
        cfg.theme, cfg.font, cfg.size, cfg.bg = DEFAULTS.theme, DEFAULTS.font, DEFAULTS.size, DEFAULTS.bg
        applyTheme(cfg.theme); applyAllText(); applyBg()
        ddTheme.Set(cfg.theme); ddFont.Set(cfg.font); slSize.Set(cfg.size); slBg.Set(cfg.bg)
        loading = false
        if hasFS and type(delfile) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                if isfile(pathFor("Shared")) then delfile(pathFor("Shared")) end
                if isfile(pathFor("PerAccount")) then delfile(pathFor("PerAccount")) end
            end)
        end
        notify("ล้างการตั้งค่าแล้ว")
    end)

    button(mg, "ทดสอบการแจ้งเตือน", function() notify("ทดสอบการแจ้งเตือน ✓") end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ปุ่มสคริปต์เดิม
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local g = group(pgSet.Left, nextOrder(), "สคริปต์", "Settings")
    button(g, "รีเฟรชรายการต้นไม้ / กองไฟ", function()
        fireCache = nil; Farm.reset(); Farm.refresh()
        notify("รีเฟรชแล้ว (ต้นไม้ " .. Farm.count() .. " ต้น)")
    end)
    button(g, "ปิดทุกฟังก์ชัน", function()
        for k, v in pairs(S) do if type(v) == "boolean" then S[k] = false end end
        stopFly(); restoreFog(); restoreBright()
        notify("ปิดทุกฟังก์ชันแล้ว (กดเปิดใหม่ได้ในแต่ละหน้า)")
    end)
    button(g, "ปิดเมนูและยกเลิกสคริปต์", function() shutdown() end)

    local i = group(pgSet.Right, nextOrder(), "เกี่ยวกับ", "Star")
    info(i, "NAPHUB · 99 คืนในป่า\nเลือกธีม/ฟอนต์/ขนาดตัวอักษรได้ในหน้านี้\nแตะปุ่มลอยเพื่อเปิด/ปิดเมนู กดค้างแล้วลากเพื่อย้าย")

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- โหลดค่าที่เซฟไว้ตอนเริ่ม แล้วใช้ทันที
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    if hasFS then
        pcall(function()
            if isfile(DIR .. "/mode.txt") then
                local m = readfile(DIR .. "/mode.txt")
                if m == "PerAccount" or m == "Shared" then saveMode = m end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local p = pathFor(saveMode)
            if isfile(p) then takeConfig(HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(p))) end
        end)
    end
    seg.Set(saveMode)
    applyTheme(cfg.theme); applyAllText(); applyBg()
    ddTheme.Set(cfg.theme); ddFont.Set(cfg.font)
    loading = true
    slSize.Set(cfg.size); slBg.Set(cfg.bg)
    loading = false
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ปุ่มปิด / ย่อ + ปุ่มลอย
----------------------------------------------------------------
minHit.Activated:Connect(function() root.Visible = false end)
closeHit.Activated:Connect(function() root.Visible = false end)

local FLOAT = 64 -- ขนาดลูกลอย (พิกเซล) ปรับได้
local floatBtn = new("ImageButton", {
    Name = "FloatButton",
    AutoButtonColor = false,
    Active = true,
    BackgroundColor3 = C.Black,
    BackgroundTransparency = logoAsset and 1 or 0,
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
    Image = logoAsset or "",
    ImageTransparency = 0,
    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch, -- แสดงรูปเต็มพื้นที่ ไม่ย่อ/ไม่ตัด
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(FLOAT, FLOAT),
    Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 90),
    ZIndex = 50,
    Parent = gui,
})
-- มุมโค้งตามกรอบในรูป (ซ่อนมุมดำของไฟล์ JPEG) · ไม่มีขอบเพิ่ม ใช้กรอบเรืองแสงของรูปเอง
corner(UDim.new(0.15, 0)).Parent = floatBtn
if not logoAsset then
    stroke(C.Line, 2).Parent = floatBtn
    label({
        Text = "N", Font = FONT, TextSize = 22, Color = C.White,
        Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = floatBtn,
    })
end

-- เอฟเฟกต์สายฟ้าดำรอบลูกลอย (ปิดได้โดยตั้ง FLOAT_LIGHTNING = false)
local FLOAT_LIGHTNING = true
if FLOAT_LIGHTNING then
    local F_GLOW = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
    local F_CORE = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

    -- เงาดำรอบปุ่ม กะพริบเบา ๆ
    local fs = new("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0.3,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = floatBtn,
    })
    TweenService:Create(fs, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {
        Transparency = 0.75,
    }):Play()

    local fLayer = new("Frame", {
        Name = "FloatLightning", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 51, Parent = floatBtn,
    })

    local function fSeg(parent, a, b, thick, color, transp)
        local d = b - a
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.BackgroundColor3 = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = transp
        f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude + thick * 0.5, thick)
        local m = (a + b) / 2
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(m.X, m.Y)
        f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
        f.Parent = parent
        return f
    end

    local fEdges = {
        { Vector2.new(0, 0),         Vector2.new(1, 0) },
        { Vector2.new(FLOAT, 0),     Vector2.new(0, 1) },
        { Vector2.new(FLOAT, FLOAT), Vector2.new(-1, 0) },
        { Vector2.new(0, FLOAT),     Vector2.new(0, -1) },
    }

    local function fPath(parent, pts, tg, tc)
        for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
            fSeg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], tg, F_GLOW, 0.55)
            fSeg(parent, pts[i], pts[i + 1], tc, F_CORE, 0)
        end
    end

    local function floatBolt()
        local e = fEdges[math.random(1, 4)]
        local start, dir = e[1], e[2]
        local normal = Vector2.new(dir.Y, -dir.X) -- ชี้ออกนอกปุ่ม
        local L = math.random(18, 34)
        local t0 = 10 + math.random() * (FLOAT - 20 - L) -- เลี่ยงมุมโค้ง
        local n = math.max(math.floor(L / 5), 4)

        local holder = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 51, Parent = fLayer,
        })
        local pts = {}
        for i = 0, n do
            local off = (i == 0 or i == n) and 1 or (math.random() * 1.6 - 0.2) * 5 -- เอนออกนอกปุ่มเป็นหลัก
            pts[i + 1] = start + dir * (t0 + L * i / n) + normal * off
        end
        fPath(holder, pts, 4, 1.2)

        if math.random() < 0.6 then
            local k = math.random(2, n)
            local bdir = (normal + dir * (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.8).Unit
            local bp = { pts[k] }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                bp[i + 1] = bp[i] + bdir * 5 + Vector2.new(math.random() * 4 - 2, math.random() * 4 - 2)
            end
            fPath(holder, bp, 3, 1)
        end

        task.delay(0.06, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = false end end)
        task.delay(0.10, function() if holder.Parent then holder.Visible = true end end)
        task.delay(0.20, function() holder:Destroy() end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while gui.Parent do
            task.wait(0.1 + math.random() * 0.25)
            if gui.Parent then
                pcall(floatBolt)
                if math.random() < 0.4 then pcall(floatBolt) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

do
    local dragging, moved = false, false
    local dragStart, startPos

    floatBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging, moved = true, false
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = floatBtn.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    if not moved then
                        root.Visible = not root.Visible
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            if d.Magnitude > 6 then moved = true end
            if moved then
                floatBtn.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
                )
            end
        end
    end))
end

selectTab("player")

pcall(function()
    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    env.NAPHUB_UI = { Gui = gui, Shutdown = shutdown, Notify = notify }
end)

return gui
